[-08-01 09:47] TEXT: 记忆里没有相关记录。基于现有代码，我先讲清楚我理解的 **LLM Wiki 逻辑**，然后逐条对照你的 5 个需求，最后再确认几个关键设计点。 --- ## 我理解的 LLM Wiki 逻辑 **核心思想**：LLM Wiki 不是 RAG（把文档切块塞进向量库），而是让 LLM 充当"知识管理员"——把零散源文档**主动整理**成一组互相链接的 markdown 页面。知识库就是磁盘上一个文件夹，页面就是里面的 `.md` 文件，纯文本、可读、可编辑、可移植。 **四层结构**（现有代码已实现雏形，见 `WikiService.ts` 的 `DEFAULT_SCHEMA`）： | 组成 | 作用 | |---|---| | `SCHEMA.md` | 知识库"宪法"：定义页面格式（YAML frontmatter）、目录分类、交叉引用规则、工作流约定 | | `index.md` | LLM 维护的索引页，链接所有页面——知识库的**入口** | | `log.md` | 操作日志，记录每次 ingest/修改 | | 页面文件 | `entities/`、`concepts/`、`sources/` 等分类目录下的 md，用 `[[双链]]` 互相关联 | **三个核心 LLM 工作流**（现有 `WikiAgentService.ts`）： 1. **Ingest（整理）**：源文档 → LLM 提取实体/概念 → 创建或更新页面 → 追加 index 链接 → 写 log。产物是**结构化的知识**而非原始片段。 2. **Query（问答）**：读 index + 相关页面 → 综合回答 → 引用来源页。回答"有据可查"。 3. **Lint（质检）**：检查页面矛盾、过时内容、孤立页面、缺失交叉引用。 **人机协作的闭环**：LLM 负责"整理 + 维护一致性"，人负责"阅读 + 补充修正"——因为底层是普通 markdown 文件，人随时能手工改。这正是它区别于黑盒 RAG 的价值。 --- ## 对照你的 5 个需求，现状与差距 | 需求 | 现状 | 差距 | |---|---|---| | 1. 多知识库，一库一大文件夹 | ✅ 已有 `wiki` 表，磁盘上每库一个目录 `{data}/wiki/{id}/` | 目录结构**写死**为 entities/concepts/sources/comparisons/synthesis 五个分类，不够自由 | | 2. 编辑知识库里的文件夹 | ❌ 页面树（`WikiPageTree.tsx`）只读，无新建/重命名/删除 | 需要新增文件夹 CRUD（IPC + UI） | | 3. 像文件系统一样编辑文件夹和 md 文件 | ⚠️ 文件可编辑（textarea），但无新建/重命名/删除/移动；目录由分类隐含 | 需要完整文件系统操作 | | 4. 索引文件体现 | ⚠️ index.md 存在，但只是普通页面，无特殊呈现，也不会随手工编辑自动维护 | 需要突出显示 + 维护机制 | | 5. LLM 能力体现 | ⚠️ ingest/query/lint 已实现，但入口分散（WikiIngestDialog / WikiQueryBar），且 ingest 只处理"源文档"而非任意页面 | 需要更显性、更贴合新目录结构 | --- 在动手前，有一个关键设计决策需要你拍板——它决定整个重构的方向：

[-08-01 09:51] TEXT: <skill_content name="compose-next"> # Compose Next Compact end-to-end contract for grill → spec → workspace → implement → verify → review → finalize → finish. One skill load, no internal skill hand-offs. ## Step 0 — Orient Inspect the repository, its instructions (`AGENTS.md`, `README`, existing spec files), and recent changes before asking anything. Do not ask the user for facts the environment already answers. Decide the shape of the work: - **Fully constrained mechanical change with no durable design surface** → skip Grill and Spec, go to Workspace then Implement. - **Requirements or design ambiguous** → Grill first. - **Requirements clear, feature deserves a durable document** → Spec first. Every path passes through Workspace before Implement; no branch skips it. ## Grill — resolve decisions Resolve one decision axis at a time. A single decision may bundle multiple dependent fields in one structured question; unrelated decisions require separate turns. Use the `question` tool for every user decision: - Put known choices in `options`. Each option gets a concise `label` and a `description` explaining the consequence. List the recommendation first and mark its label `(Recommended)`. - For consequential choices, include 2–3 viable alternatives. - When choices cannot be enumerated, still call `question` with `options: []` for free-text. - Do not ask for permission to continue when no decision remains. Split requests spanning independent subsystems before refining each part. Do not begin implementation until requirements and scope are settled. ### Never-Ask handling If the `question` tool is unavailable or returns `[Never-Ask]`, resolve **this one decision** yourself and continue: 1. Choose the option marked `(Recommended)` when repository evidence still supports it and it can run unattended. 2. Otherwise choose the closest minimal-scope option supported by the evidence; prefer text-only, non-interactive work. 3. If the decision includes destructive or irreversible work, choose a non-destructive path that preserves progress; never auto-approve the destructive option. 4. State the option selected and the reason in the response. Never-Ask applies to the current decision only. At every later decision point, call the `question` tool again — Never-Ask does not disable future questions or pause the workflow. ## Spec — one document per feature Maintain one document per feature at `docs/compose/spec/<feature-name>.md` from the repository root. Do not add a date to the filename. A user-specified location overrides this path. Edit an existing document in place; never create a separate plan or report. ### Template ```markdown --- feature: <feature-name> status: designed | in-progress | delivered updated: YYYY-MM-DD branch: <branch-name> commits: <base-sha>..<head-sha> # filled at delivery --- # <Feature Name> ## Report ## [S1] Problem Describe the user-visible problem. ## [S2] Design Record the chosen behavior and the contracts needed to implement it. ## [S3] Out of Scope State explicit boundaries. ## Tasks - [ ] T1: <work item> — acceptance: <observable result> (covers: S2) - [ ] T2: <work item> — acceptance: <observable result> (covers: S2; depends: T1) ``` ### Design-time rules - Leave `Report` empty and set `status: designed`. - Keep `[Sn]` anchors stable when headings change; never renumber existing anchors. - Record settled decisions and precise contracts, not exploration history or file-level code dumps. Include architecture, interfaces, data …(+8361)

[-08-01 09:52] TEXT: 读完了 Karpathy 的原文。我之前的理解确实偏差了，核心问题在于：**我把 LLM wiki 当成了一个"整理工具"，而原文的核心是"分工模式"**。重新讲一遍： --- ## 修正后的 LLM Wiki 理解 ### 1. 与 RAG 的本质区别：**复利式积累**（compounding artifact） RAG 每次提问都从原始文档重新拼凑知识——"没有积累"。LLM Wiki 相反：每次 ingest 时 LLM **把知识编译进 wiki 一次，然后持续保鲜**（update entity pages、revise summaries、标记新旧数据矛盾、强化/挑战综合结论）。交叉引用已就位、矛盾已标记、综合已反映全部阅读——wiki 每加一个源、每问一个问题就更丰富。**这是全文最重要的论点，我上一版完全没讲透。** ### 2. 三层架构，职责严格分离 | 层 | 谁拥有 | 性质 | |---|---|---| | **Raw sources** | 人类 | 不可变、只读，是 source of truth。LLM 读但永不改 | | **The wiki** | **LLM 独占** | 全部由 LLM 写和维护。人类**从不（或极少）自己写 wiki** | | **The schema** | 人机共演进 | 领域专属的配置文件，随使用不断演化——"正是它让 LLM 成为纪律严明的 wiki 维护者而非通用聊天机器人" | > 原文原话："You never (or rarely) write the wiki yourself — the LLM writes and maintains all of it. You're in charge of sourcing, exploration, and asking the right questions." > Obsidian 是 IDE，LLM 是程序员，wiki 是代码库。**一次 ingest 可能触碰 10-15 个页面。** ### 3. 三个操作，其中有个我漏掉的闭环 - **Ingest**：读源 → 与你讨论要点 → 写摘要页 → 更新 index → 跨 wiki 更新实体/概念页 → 追加 log。可单源精读、可批量粗读。 - **Query**：先读 index 定位 → 读相关页 → 综合回答并引用。**关键洞见：好的回答要"归档回 wiki"成为新页面**——对比表、分析、发现的联系不该消失在聊天历史里。探索像 ingest 一样复利积累。← 我漏了这条 - **Lint**：健康检查——矛盾、过时声明、无入站链接的孤儿页、提到但缺页的概念、缺交叉引用、可搜索补充的数据空白。还能建议新问题新来源。 ### 4. index.md 和 log.md 是导航双轨，用途不同 - **index.md 面向内容**：全库目录——每页链接 + 一行摘要 + 元数据，按类别组织。**LLM 回答问题时先读 index 再下钻**——这就是替代 RAG 的扩展策略（~100 源、数百页规模够用）。 - **log.md 面向时间**：append-only 流水账，固定前缀 `## [2026-04-02] ingest | 标题`，可用 `grep "^## \[" log.md | tail -5` 解析。← 前缀约定是个实用细节 ### 5. 为什么成立 + 配套生态 - 维护成本趋近于零 → 人类弃坑的"书签维护负担"被 LLM 消化；**人的工作是选源、定方向、提问、思考意义**。 - wiki 就是 git 仓库的 markdown → 天然版本历史、分支、协作。 - 可选 CLI 工具（qmd 混合 BM25/向量搜索）在 index 不够用时的扩展路径。 - 精神源头：Vannevar Bush 的 Memex（1945）——私人、主动策展、**文档间的联系与文档本身等值**。 --- ## 对照你的需求，修正后的差距分析 | 需求 | 现在看清的问题 | |---|---| | 1. 多知识库一库一文件夹 | ✅ 天然匹配——wiki 就是"目录树 + 一堆 md"。但注意：**源文件(raw)和 wiki 产物要分目录**（raw 不可变） | | 2/3. 人工编辑文件夹和 md | ⚠️ **这是对原文模式的有意扩展**——原文说"人从不写 wiki"，但你要求"像文件系统一样可编辑"。可行：wiki 是纯 markdown，Obsidian 模式就是人工可改。**关键是"人改完之后，LLM 维护层怎么感知"**——这是必须设计的核心问题 | | 4. 索引文件体现 | index.md 不该只是普通页面——它是 LLM 的查询入口 + 人的导航目录，需要**突出呈现 + ingest 后自动维护**（对应原文"LLM 每次 ingest 都更新 index"） | | 5. LLM 能力体现 | 不只是 ingest/query/lint 三个按钮——还要体现：**回答归档回 wiki**（query→新页面）、**ingest 时"讨论要点"环节**、schema 可视化编辑（人机共演进）、交叉引用/图谱可见性（Obsidian graph 的对应物） | --- 下一个关键设计决策——它直接决定"人工编辑"和"LLM 维护"如何共处：

[-08-01 13:17] TEXT: <skill_content name="compose-next"> # Compose Next Compact end-to-end contract for grill → spec → workspace → implement → verify → review → finalize → finish. One skill load, no internal skill hand-offs. ## Step 0 — Orient Inspect the repository, its instructions (`AGENTS.md`, `README`, existing spec files), and recent changes before asking anything. Do not ask the user for facts the environment already answers. Decide the shape of the work: - **Fully constrained mechanical change with no durable design surface** → skip Grill and Spec, go to Workspace then Implement. - **Requirements or design ambiguous** → Grill first. - **Requirements clear, feature deserves a durable document** → Spec first. Every path passes through Workspace before Implement; no branch skips it. ## Grill — resolve decisions Resolve one decision axis at a time. A single decision may bundle multiple dependent fields in one structured question; unrelated decisions require separate turns. Use the `question` tool for every user decision: - Put known choices in `options`. Each option gets a concise `label` and a `description` explaining the consequence. List the recommendation first and mark its label `(Recommended)`. - For consequential choices, include 2–3 viable alternatives. - When choices cannot be enumerated, still call `question` with `options: []` for free-text. - Do not ask for permission to continue when no decision remains. Split requests spanning independent subsystems before refining each part. Do not begin implementation until requirements and scope are settled. ### Never-Ask handling If the `question` tool is unavailable or returns `[Never-Ask]`, resolve **this one decision** yourself and continue: 1. Choose the option marked `(Recommended)` when repository evidence still supports it and it can run unattended. 2. Otherwise choose the closest minimal-scope option supported by the evidence; prefer text-only, non-interactive work. 3. If the decision includes destructive or irreversible work, choose a non-destructive path that preserves progress; never auto-approve the destructive option. 4. State the option selected and the reason in the response. Never-Ask applies to the current decision only. At every later decision point, call the `question` tool again — Never-Ask does not disable future questions or pause the workflow. ## Spec — one document per feature Maintain one document per feature at `docs/compose/spec/<feature-name>.md` from the repository root. Do not add a date to the filename. A user-specified location overrides this path. Edit an existing document in place; never create a separate plan or report. ### Template ```markdown --- feature: <feature-name> status: designed | in-progress | delivered updated: YYYY-MM-DD branch: <branch-name> commits: <base-sha>..<head-sha> # filled at delivery --- # <Feature Name> ## Report ## [S1] Problem Describe the user-visible problem. ## [S2] Design Record the chosen behavior and the contracts needed to implement it. ## [S3] Out of Scope State explicit boundaries. ## Tasks - [ ] T1: <work item> — acceptance: <observable result> (covers: S2) - [ ] T2: <work item> — acceptance: <observable result> (covers: S2; depends: T1) ``` ### Design-time rules - Leave `Report` empty and set `status: designed`. - Keep `[Sn]` anchors stable when headings change; never renumber existing anchors. - Record settled decisions and precise contracts, not exploration history or file-level code dumps. Each file should have one clear responsibility. - You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more reliable when files are focused. Prefer smaller, focused files over large ones that do too much. - Files that change together should live together. Split by responsibility, not by technical layer. - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. If the codebase uses large files, don't unilaterally restructure - but if a file you're modifying has grown unwieldy, including a split in the plan is reasonable. This structure informs the task decomposition. Each task should produce self-contained changes that make sense independently. ## Task Right-Sizing A task is the smallest unit that carries its own test cycle and is worth a fresh reviewer's gate. When drawing task boundaries: - Fold setup, configuration, scaffolding, and documentation steps into the task whose deliverable needs them - Split only where a reviewer could meaningfully reject one task while approving its neighbor - Each task ends with an independently testable deliverable ## Bite-Sized Task Granularity **Each step is one action (2-5 minutes):** - "Write the failing test" - step - "Run it to make sure it fails" - step - "Implement the minimal code to make the test pass" - step - "Run the tests and make sure they pass" - step - "Commit" - step ## Plan Document Header **Every plan MUST start with this header:** ```markdown # [Feature Name] Implementation Plan > **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use compose:subagent (recommended) or compose:execute to implement this plan task-by-task. [-08-01 13:26] TEXT: `RichEditor` 是给 Composer 用的**富文本（WYSIWYG）编辑器**，不适合直接编辑 .md 源文件（会破坏 LLM 读写的 markdown 源）。仓库另有 `markdown` 渲染组件（已在用）。所以编辑方向是**增强版 Markdown 编辑**（Obsidian 源码模式），而不是富文本。 基于你的方向 + A–E 五个问题，我规划的目标设计如下： ## 目标页面逻辑（知识库软件式） **左侧面板** = 知识库切换器（顶部下拉）+ 完整文件树（Obsidian 式） - 树顶层直接是**页面内容**：文件夹 + md 文件平铺（`raw/` 源区、`SCHEMA.md` 从主树隐藏，源文件走"导入"入口管理） - index.md 仍置顶（目录卡片入口），log.md 置底 **右侧** = 工具栏（导入 / 整理 / 质检 / Schema / 同步）+ 编辑器 **核心逻辑改进**： 1. **导入文件**（新能力）：工具栏"导入"→ ①导入源文档（进 raw/ 供 Ingest 整理）②直接导入为页面（放入选中文件夹）；**拖拽文件到树上 = 导入为页面** 2. **编辑**：自动保存（停止输入 1s）+ "已保存/保存中"状态 + 同步索引指示器（Wiki_Synced 事件驱动）；切页不丢改动 3. **知识库管理闭环**：重命名知识库、删除时**连磁盘文件夹一起删**（主进程改动） 4. **导航**：选中知识库不强制开空 index——新库显示引导卡片，已有页面时开 index 卡片视图 5. **空状态引导**：新库 → "导入源文档 / 新建文件夹 / 新建文件"三个动作引导 6. **文件操作**：右键菜单补"移动/复制到…"（跨文件夹） 三个关键设计决策确认一下：

[-08-01 14:44] TEXT: 修复完成并已提交。总结： ## 根因（两个叠加 bug） **① 幽灵页（主因）**：`handleCreateWiki` 创建新知识库时**没有重置 `selectedPagePath`**——上一个知识库打开的页面路径残留。新建知识库后视图卡在一个"幽灵空页面"（新库里不存在该文件 → 读取失败），引导卡不显示；此时在树里点同名文件（如 `新页面.md`）路径相同 → WikiPageView 不重新加载 → 看起来"点了进不去"。 **② auto-select 竞态**：自动打开 index.md 的逻辑在**旧树**（上一个库的树，有内容）上提前触发，导致空库也打开了空 index 而非引导卡。 ## 修复（`KnowledgePage.tsx`，已提交 master `0e4d1f9`） 1. `handleCreateWiki` 创建后 `setSelectedPagePath('')`（与切换库一致）→ 新库正确显示引导卡 2. 新增 `treeWikiId` 状态：auto-select 只在**当前库的树加载完成**后才触发（防旧树竞态） 3. 体验改进：引导卡"新建文件"创建后**直接选中打开该文件** ## 验证（CDP 端到端复现） 新建知识库 → ✅ 引导卡显示 → 点"新建文件" → ✅ 文件出现在树中且**直接打开进入编辑**（右侧显示 `新页面.md / 编辑`）→ 无控制台报错。修复前该流程复现出幽灵页 bug，修复后连续两轮通过。 ## 收尾 - 测试数据已清理（只剩你的 "AI" 知识库）；临时 CDP 脚本已删 - 应用已从**主 checkout** 以正常 `pnpm dev` 重启（无调试端口、无 pnpm 阻塞），现在就能直接测试 - 关键教训（多选型 state 切换资源须源头重置 + auto-select 数据归属守卫）已记入项目记忆
